Read setup/github-sync/README.md, .cloud-sync/workspace.json, and the relevant .cloud-sync/conversations/index.json and handoff. Resolve paths against your actual cloned repositories. Never assume a local Windows path exists here. Work on a new task branch; do not push edits to a local-sync branch. Fetch the latest sync branches before starting and check recorded source/freshness metadata. Report missing dependencies and test actual live tool access before production changes.\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Operations\\r\\n\\r\\n```powershell\\r\\npython setup/github-sync/sync.py --once --dry-run --config setup/github-sync/config.json\\r\\npython setup/github-sync/run_sync.py --once --config setup/github-sync/config.json\\r\\npowershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File setup/github-sync/install.ps1\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nGenerated state is machine-only in `setup/github-sync/state/`. `status.json` records GitHub results; `drive-status.json` records verified Drive uploads; `runner-status.json` records the combined run. Inspect these for conflicts, excluded files, failed credential checks, or upload errors. New project folders are discovered automatically. Folders without a remote receive a new private `local-<folder>` repository under the configured owner; existing name collisions and public or foreign repositories block publication for review.\\r\\n\\r\\nGitleaks 8.30.1 was obtained from its official GitHub release. Report: system/generated/wiki-lint.generated.tsv\\")\\r\\n    print(\\"Semantic contradictions, stale claims, and knowledge gaps still require a source-grounded review.\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef main():\\r\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\\r\\n    parser.add_argument(\\"--index\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\r\\n    parser.add_argument(\\"--lint\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\r\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\r\\n    if not args.index and not args.lint:\\r\\n        parser.error(\\"Choose --index, --lint, or both\\")\\r\\n    pages = sorted(WIKI.rglob(\\"*.md\\"))\\r\\n    if args.index:\\r\\n        catalog(pages)\\r\\n    if args.lint:\\r\\n        lint(pages)\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nif __name__ == \\"__main__\\":\\r\\n    main()\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f011b2","wall_time_seconds":0.3853971,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2443,"output":"---\\r\\ntitle: Students — one folder per student\\r\\ntype: operating-layer\\r\\ntags: [students, operating-layer]\\r\\ncreated: 2026-07-03\\r\\nupdated: 2026-09-25\\r\\nstatus: active\\r\\nsubject: Students - one folder per student\\r\\naliases: []\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Students\\r\\n\\r\\nOne folder per student, holding **everything** about them (D2, locked 2026-07-16): transcripts, correspondence, homework, notes, schedules, profile. These are internal working records for Joshua, Claude, and Codex, not pages for students to open. Open the student\'s folder and their whole working record is there. This is one of the brain\'s three knowledge systems, beside [[../wiki/index|wiki]] and [[../corpus/index|corpus]].\\r\\n\\r\\n## Current students\\r\\n\\r\\n- [[adriana-varea/adriana-varea|Adriana Varea]] · [[amanda/amanda|Amanda]] · [[asia/asia|Asia]] · [[avneet-natt/avneet-natt|Avneet Natt]] · [[celia-cutler/celia-cutler|Celia Cutler]] · [[charles-moore/charles-moore|Charles Moore]] · [[dakota/dakota|Dakota]] · [[david-salgado/david-salgado|David Salgado]] · [[ethan/ethan|Ethan]] · [[favour/favour|Favour]] · [[fernande-charleus/fernande-charleus|Fernande Charleus]] · [[gianna/gianna|Gianna Jean]] · [[ivory-johnson/ivory-johnson|Ivory Johnson]] · [[nicole/nicole|Nicole]] · [[ore/ore|Ore]] · [[sandra/sandra|Sandra Kooh]] · [[sofia/sofia|Sofia]] · [[steven-morris/steven-morris|Steven Morris]] · [[venusa-laveaux/venusa-laveaux|Venusa Laveaux]] · [[vinay-kalva/vinay-kalva|Vinay Kalva]] · [[whit-wilson/whit-wilson|Whit Wilson]] · [[yahunatan-zetvy/yahunatan-zetvy|Yahunatan Zetvy]] · [[zeina-borji/zeina-borji|Zeina Borji]]\\r\\n\\r\\n**Taught in the last month** (2026-08-08 to 2026-09-08, transcripts pulled 2026-09-08): Celia Cutler (6), Adriana Varea (5), Steven Morris (4), Sofia (2), Vinay Kalva (2), Yahunatan Zetvy (2), Asia (1), Charles Moore (1), Fernande Charleus (1), Whit Wilson (1).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Former students\\r\\n\\r\\nLive in [[non-students/CLAUDE|non-students/]] as of 2026-09-10. Records preserved, excluded from every sweep. Read that folder\'s `CLAUDE.md` before touching anything in it.\\r\\n\\r\\n## The primary/mirror boundary (D4 — read before regenerating anything)\\r\\n\\r\\nEvery file in a student folder is one of two kinds, and the distinction decides what a rebuild may overwrite:\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Primary — the brain is the only home.** Transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, study plans, anything Joshua or an agent authored about the student. **Never regenerate, never overwrite; retain forever.**\\r\\n- **Mirror — a projection of a live backend.** Session/booking state (Cal.com + Google Calendar), homework/dossier/publish state (LSAT U Supabase), payment events (Stripe), correspondence indexes (Gmail). On disagreement, the verified backend governs operational state. Preserve and report the discrepancy with its evidence rather than silently erasing a conflicting primary record.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Convention:** mirror files MUST be named `*.generated.md` (or carry `generated: true` frontmatter). A file without that marker is primary by definition and must be treated as precious. Pre-2026-07-16 files (`email-context.md`, `payment-links.md`, `transcripts.md`) predate the marker — treat as primary until individually re-derived and renamed. No Markdown file here is ever the source of operational truth — no package balances, no running totals (all services are paid upfront; Cal.com answers sessions-used).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Standard files per student folder\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is the target layout, not a checklist to satisfy with empty files. Create a record when its source or a real teaching judgment supports it. Leave an absent record absent until there is verified content to put in it.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `<student-slug>.md` — internal entry page and folder router (primary). Its `## Open this record` block links only to local files that exist.\\r\\n- `email-context.md` — synthesized Gmail context.\\r\\n- `email-correspondence.md` — Gmail message-id corpus for the student\'s confirmed address.\\r\\n- `payment-links.md` — verified payment, billing, and scheduling links.\\r\\n- `transcripts.md` — transcript and meeting-note inventory. Its `## Local session files` table links directly to every full local Markdown export and every session package, newest first. A Google Doc link is provenance, never the only way to read a session.\\r\\n- `teaching-notes.md` — durable, evidence-backed learning judgments and teaching patterns (primary).\\r\\n- `open-loops.md` — unfinished follow-ups and pending decisions (primary).\\r\\n- `live-status.generated.md` — currently a refreshable LSAT U booking-pass mirror. Calendar, Gmail, and payment state remain in their live systems; this file does not yet summarize them.\\r\\n- `transcript-downloads/` — preserved local exports of Gemini notes (source evidence; do not hand-edit). A raw export here is not itself a completed session package.\\r\\n- `sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-name/` — new self-contained session packages.\\r\\n- `assignment-log.md` — running log of assigned work and notification evidence so repeats are avoided (primary).\\r\\n- `Practice_Test_Reports/` — the student\'s PT score reports as received (intake form, screenshots, exports). Raw evidence — read for diagnosis, never edit.\\r\\n- `Student_Specific_Homework/` — work assigned to this student specifically. Distinct from the shared banks in `corpus/Curriculum/*/Homework/`, which are the source material it draws from.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Conventions\\r\\n\\r\\n### Connect a student observation to shared teaching knowledge\\r\\n\\r\\nUse [tutoring diagnosis](../corpus/tutoring-diagnosis.md) as a hypothesis framework when reviewing an actual session or score report. Keep what the person said or did, Joshua\'s judgment and the resulting follow-up in their own teaching notes and open loops. Link the appropriate shared [corpus method](../corpus/index.md), including the recovered [RC recall check](<../corpus/Curriculum/Reading Comprehension/Notes/cumulative-translation-review.md>) when evidence supports it. Apply [Joshua\'s teaching voice standard](../references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md) to student-facing adaptations. A general source never establishes a diagnosis, assignment or commitment by itself.\\r\\n\\r\\n- New student → new folder `students/<slug>/` with a `<slug>.md` profile page.\\r\\n- A consultation followed by a subsequent student session establishes onboarding. Check the Calendar sequence and identity before creating the folder; record any uncertainty about attendance or contact aliases. Consultation-only records stay in `_other-sessions/`.\\r\\n- When Joshua explicitly says a student relationship is over or to close a folder, move the preserved record to `students/non-students/<slug>/`, remove it from the current student index, and refresh the generated roster. Do not delete the record or silently infer closure from inactivity.\\r\\n- New session → `sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-name/` containing `transcript.md`, `summary.md`, and `topic-map.md`.\\r\\n- Keep the original full Gemini export as a local Markdown file in `transcript-downloads/` when available. Build the session package from verified session evidence, and link both the export and package from `transcripts.md`; older exports may predate the package convention.\\r\\n- `transcript.md` preserves the raw source. `summary.md` records the auto-saved summary, action items, and appended corrections. `topic-map.md` is a timestamped link index to relevant LSAT concepts, student records, and source material.\\r\\n- Session summaries are append-only. Later corrections go in a dated correction section and never erase the original summary.\\r\\n- Generated operational state is clearly marked with `generated: true` and never overrides primary teaching records.\\r\\n- Wikilink student pages into corpus concepts and LSAT U material as they become relevant.\\r\\n- Tier A note: transcripts and correspondence are trackable (Tier-A amendment, 2026-07-16). True secrets never enter Git: credentials and bank/account information belong in the git-ignored root `.env`; government IDs stay outside this OS. There is no `private/` storage tier. See [current handling policy](../wiki/HANDLING.md).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Internal routing contract after each session\\r\\n\\r\\n1. Match the Calendar event and Gemini Doc by guest email, date, and content. Preserve the complete source export in `transcript-downloads/`; never rely on a Drive link as the record.\\r\\n2. Create or update the session\'s `sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-name/` package without replacing a primary transcript or summary. Record any source mismatch as a dated correction.\\r\\n3. Add a newest-first row to `transcripts.md` under `## Local session files`: date, direct local full-export link, local package links, and source/status. Include raw-only historical exports and packages even when the other half is absent.\\r\\n4. Update evidence-backed `teaching-notes.md` and `open-loops.md`. Refresh the student\'s `<slug>.md` `## Open this record` block so Joshua, Claude, and Codex can reach the latest work in two clicks. Link only to files that exist.\\r\\n5. Check that all local links resolve, that the full Markdown export contains the transcript rather than only Gemini\'s summary, and that the session index has no unlinked export or package. Leave attendance and missing-source uncertainty explicit.\\r\\n6. Run `python system/scripts/check-student-records.py` from the repo root before calling intake complete. It checks all student routers and every local Markdown export and session package link.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Operating contract\\r\\n\\r\\nFor each new consultation or transcript, Jay 2 must report what was found, what was created or updated, what was inferred, what remains missing, what is recommended, and what Joshua should confirm or reject. During the learning period, no student-facing message, assignment, or platform action is implied by record preparation alone.\\r\\n"}'}]

        packages += 1\\r\\n            for name in (\\"transcript.md\\", \\"summary.md\\", \\"topic-map.md\\"):\\r\\n                file = package / name\\r\\n                if not ## Tool: function_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c3786c","wall_time_seconds":0.366039,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1308,"output":"---\\r\\ntype: teaching-notes\\r\\nsubject: Corpus LSAT teaching knowledge\\r\\nupdated: 2026-07-17\\r\\naliases: []\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Corpus — LSAT teaching knowledge\\r\\n\\r\\nThe second knowledge system: Joshua\'s LSAT teaching knowledge, built incrementally on the Karpathy LLM-wiki pattern as materials are ingested (books + notes, LSAT Lab, 7Sage material, drills, explanations). Eventually integrates into LSAT U as platform feedstock.\\r\\n\\r\\nFills through supervised ingests, one source at a time, logged in [[../wiki/log|the wiki log]]. Raw materials land in `raw/` first (immutable); the corpus holds the synthesized, interlinked teaching pages.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Conventions\\r\\n\\r\\n- Pages are LLM-written and LLM-maintained; Joshua curates sources and directs emphasis.\\r\\n- Wikilink corpus concepts into `students/<name>/` pages when a student is working that area, and into LSAT U material as it ships.\\r\\n- Question-type taxonomy, method pages, and drill-design principles get their own pages as they accumulate — no empty scaffolding in advance.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Pages\\r\\n\\r\\n### Teaching methods recovered from the old local vault (2026-10-07)\\r\\n\\r\\n- [Loophole teacher reference](loophole-online/index.md): translation, quipping, CLIR, question strategies and review routines, with attributed source navigation and defective-capture warnings.\\r\\n- [Loophole operational method library](loophole-online/method-library.md): detailed instructor protocols, source-specific targets and doctrine checks, connected to four annotated editions retaining captured lecture explanations and worked examples.\\r\\n- [Diagnose the cause of a miss](tutoring-diagnosis.md): the recovered 7Sage four-category coaching framework and whole-section timing review, retained as a private teacher reference.\\r\\n- [RC cumulative translation](<Curriculum/Reading Comprehension/Notes/cumulative-translation-review.md>): a concrete recall-check protocol for an observed reading-retention problem, filed in its topic home.\\r\\n- [Source and comparison](../wiki/sources/old-obsidian-vault-2026-10-07.md): preservation hashes, full local-file coverage, missing images and historical-policy conflicts.\\r\\n\\r\\n### `lectures/` · read-aloud scripts and teaching visuals\\r\\n\\r\\n- [[lectures/index]] · LSAT Lab source transcripts, teleprompter scripts, initial concept boards, and worked-question visuals. Packages preserve source timestamps and explicit completeness checks.\\r\\n\\r\\n### `Curriculum/` — the LR curriculum (ingested 2026-07-17)\\r\\n\\r\\n118 markdown pages recovered from `raw/sources/lsat-curriculum/_stragglers/local-ai-gather/…/Curriculum/` — the corpus folder tree had been scaffolded from this source in the 2026-07-16 rebuild, but the files themselves were never copied. Each topic carries `Notes/` (the teaching) and `Homework/` (the assigned drills).\\r\\n\\r\\n| Topic | Pages | Topic | Pages |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| Main Conclusion | 21 | Necessary Assumption | 7 |\\r\\n| Flaw and Famous Flaws | 11 | Conditional Logic | 7 |\\r\\n| Abstract | 6 | Causation | 6 |\\r\\n| Principle | 4 | Role | 4 |\\r\\n| Sufficient Assumption | 4 | Must be True | 3 |\\r\\n| Strengthen | 2 | Parallel | 2 |\\r\\n| Rebuttals | 2 | Comparison | 1 |\\r\\n| Method | 1 | Most Supported | 1 |\\r\\n| Parallel Flaw | 1 | | |\\r\\n\\r\\n- `Curriculum/_question-feature-index/` — 33 pages indexing question features across PTs 101–159 (famous-flaws, complex-conditionals, 2-speaker, science, except, …).\\r\\n- `Curriculum/_meta/lr-taxonomy.md` — the LR question-type taxonomy.\\r\\n- `Curriculum/LR/` — 2 homework pages exported live from Google Drive (pilot, 2026-07-17).\\r\\n\\r\\n**Known gaps:** topics scaffolded but still unfilled — Agree Disagree, Bootcamp, Match Family, Must Be False, Paradox, Quantifiers, Weaken, Reading Comprehension. Their source material is split between `raw/sources/lsat-curriculum/LSAT DUMP/` (many entries are stale `.gdoc` Drive pointers whose IDs no longer resolve) and Google Drive itself, where a past migration converted docs to PDFs/scans under new IDs. Recovering those needs a Drive-side pass, not a local copy.\\r\\n\\r\\n2026-10-07 recovery note: the [RC cumulative-translation protocol](<Curriculum/Reading Comprehension/Notes/cumulative-translation-review.md>) now provides one attributed method in the RC topic home. It does not establish recovery of the complete RC curriculum. Conditional and objection topic pages now link the relevant recovered evidence without replacing their current teaching standards.\\r\\n\\r\\n### `lr-explanations/` — models for writing LR explanations (2026-09-26)\\r\\n\\r\\n- [[lr-explanations/golden-set]] — the 51 LSAT Lab explanations new LSAT U explanations are modelled on, with the quality rubric.\\r\\n- [[lr-explanations/models/index]] — model library: 334 top-tier existing explanations, 10 to 20 per LR type, with what the best ones do for each type.\\r\\n- [[lr-explanations/drafts/pilot-2026-09-27]] — 20-question pilot of new explanations, waiting for Joshua.\\r\\n- [[lr-explanations/golden-set-observations]] — strong-vs-loose comparison, step structure per type, voice notes.\\r\\n- [[lr-explanations/golden-set-candidates]] — all 80 candidates and the type x feature coverage grid.\\r\\n- [[lr-explanations/fixes/index]] — the fix list: one line per issue, with detail files beside it (revamp list, Choice Note issues).\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script running with cell ID 11\nWall time 31.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"da5333","wall_time_seconds":0.378299,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1246,"output":"---\\r\\nname: ingest\\r\\ndescription: >\\r\\n  The single front door for adding sources to the current knowledge base. Use\\r\\n  for \\"ingest this\\", \\"process this source\\", \\"file these notes\\", \\"add this to\\r\\n  my second brain\\", \\"ingest the latest clips\\", or a supplied document meant\\r\\n  to become saved knowledge. Finds the input, checks content hashes, preserves\\r\\n  provenance, and compiles it into linked wiki pages. Session write-ups and\\r\\n  student-facing post-session recaps belong to post-session-recap.\\r\\nallowed-tools: Bash Read Write Edit Glob Grep\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Ingest sources into the current brain\\r\\n\\r\\nOne public skill owns discovery, duplicate checks, preservation, routing, and\\r\\nwiki compilation. `second-brain-ingest` is a compatibility name for this same\\r\\nworkflow, not another pipeline. Read the internal\\r\\n`references/wiki-compilation.md` when processing knowledge sources.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Resolve the vault and schema first\\r\\n\\r\\nFind the vault root from the current workspace and its nearest instruction files.\\r\\nRead `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` relative to that root. It is the authority for\\r\\nfolder conventions, page metadata, provenance, index, log, and ingest/query/lint\\r\\nworkflows. Do not assume a saved Windows or Mac path. If no schema exists in the\\r\\ntarget vault, report that gap before changing its structure; never initialize a\\r\\nsecond vault to solve a request about an existing one.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Route first\\r\\n\\r\\nIdentify what is being ingested, then jump to that section. If it is genuinely\\r\\nambiguous, ask once. Do not guess and file it somewhere wrong.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Input | Where it lands | Section |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| Web clip from Obsidian Web Clipper (`.md` in `raw/inbox/`) | `raw/inbox/` | [Clips](#clips) |\\r\\n| A clip that is a YouTube / video page | `raw/inbox/` | [Clips](#clips), read via `watch` |\\r\\n| Gemini \\"Notes by Gemini\\" from a live session (this week, last week) | Google Drive | [Transcripts, live](#transcripts-live) |\\r\\n| Archival Gemini `.docx` / `.txt` (Apr to Jul 2026 Takeout batch) | `raw/data-about-me/transcripts/` | [Transcripts, archival](#transcripts-archival) |\\r\\n| Superwhisper `recordings/` dirs (`meta.json` + `output.wav`) or `settings.json` | wherever they were dropped | hand to `superwhisper`; not this skill\'s |\\r\\n| Student evidence: PT score report, screenshot, homework export | `~/Downloads`, chat attachment | [Student evidence](#student-evidence) |\\r\\n| A source document meant to become wiki knowledge | `raw/` | dedupe below, then read `references/wiki-compilation.md` |\\r\\n| Takeout or platform dump | `raw/data-about-me/` | [Bulk exports](#bulk-exports) |\\r\\n\\r\\nKnowledge-source compilation is this skill\'s internal reference. `post-session-recap`\\r\\nowns the complete student-facing session write-up workflow. A request only to\\r\\nretrieve or file a transcript stays narrow; a recap request goes directly to that\\r\\nskill without asking for a second activation.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Always, before any pipeline\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **Find the file without asking where it is.** Search newest-first:\\r\\n   ```\\r\\n   raw/inbox/   raw/sources/   ~/Downloads/   ~/Desktop/   students/*/transcript-downloads/\\r\\n   ```\\r\\n   Ask only if that comes up empty. He has answered \\"where is it\\" too many\\r\\n   times. `raw/inbox/` is the single intake queue; the old top-level\\r\\n   `Clippings/` folder was folded into `raw/sources/clips/` on 2026-09-08, so a\\r\\n   `Clippings/` path in an old note is stale.\\r\\n2. **Dedupe by SHA-256, not by name.** On Windows use `Get-FileHash` or\\r\\n   Python `hashlib`. Check existing archive manifests, source-page metadata,\\r\\n   and dated ingest entries. A matching filename alone is not proof. A matching\\r\\n   hash with completed wiki output means report the existing output and stop.\\r\\n   An archived hash without finished output means resume compilation, not\\r\\n   discard the source. Never delete duplicates.\\r\\n3. **Check the log and source summary.** Record whether the candidate was\\r\\n   previously processed and whether this is a new revision. Preserve both\\r\\n   generations when content changed. A deliberate re-ingest updates the\\r\\n   existing source page with revision provenance, not a duplicate truth.\\r\\n4. **Preserve the input byte-for-byte.** Copy external attachments or inbox\\r\\n   inputs to an appropriate `raw/sources/` archive and verify the SHA-256 before\\r\\n   citing the preserved copy. Record origin, URL/author/date when known,\\r\\n   capture date, archived date, filename, size, and hash. Do not move, delete,\\r\\n   overwrite, or edit original source bytes. Do not follow symlinks outside a\\r\\n   registered source root. Existing archived inputs can be read in place.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Clips\\r\\n\\r\\nObsidian Web Clipper writes `.md` files to `raw/inbox/`. Anything else sitting\\r\\nthere (a `recordings/` tree, a `settings.json`) is not a clip; route it by the\\r\\ntable above and tell Joshua it was in the wrong place rather than processing it\\r\\nhere.\\r\\n\\r\\n1. List `raw/inbox/*.md` newest-first. \\"The latest\\" means the newest; a named\\r\\n   topic means match on it; several new ones means list them and ask which.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"470c1f","wall_time_seconds":0.3473315,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":588,"output":"# Inbox\\r\\n\\r\\nThe one intake queue. Everything unprocessed lands here and nothing else does.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is the intended destination for Obsidian Web Clipper templates. The browser\'s\\r\\nselected template controls its actual save location; verify that it names this\\r\\nvault and `raw/inbox/`. Some recent clips still exist in the legacy top-level\\r\\n`Clippings/` folder. Downloads and other one-off sources can be dropped here too.\\r\\n\\r\\n## The contract\\r\\n\\r\\nA file sitting here is an intake candidate. Its presence alone does not establish\\r\\nwhether it has already been processed, because preserved originals remain here.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen Joshua asks to ingest a clip, `ingest` checks its content hash and prior\\r\\noutputs, copies and hash-verifies it into `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` with\\r\\n`PROVENANCE.md` and `SHA256SUMS`, reads it, and compiles useful content into the\\r\\nexisting owning pages in wiki, corpus, references, context or student records.\\r\\nIt updates the source summary, indexes and `wiki/log.md`, checks links and\\r\\ncontradictions, and reports gaps. It does not move or delete the original by\\r\\ndefault. Copying a clip here does not itself run ingestion.\\r\\n\\r\\nProcessed status comes from hashes, linked source summaries and ingest log\\r\\nentries, not whether the inbox is empty. A duplicate already compiled source\\r\\ndoes not need another knowledge page. See `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` and the\\r\\nactive `ingest` skill for the governing workflow.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Why it works this way\\r\\n\\r\\nUntil 2026-09-08 there were two inboxes: this one, which was never used, and a\\r\\ntop-level `Clippings/` folder that the Web Clipper wrote to and that nothing ever\\r\\nemptied. Clips accumulated there for two months, including three byte-identical\\r\\ncopies of the same video. `Clippings/` was folded into `raw/sources/clips/` and\\r\\nretired as the intended intake route. As checked on 2026-10-08, three clips\\r\\nremain in that legacy folder, so the browser template destination still needs\\r\\nverification. The current ingest rules require copying rather than moving.\\r\\n\\r\\nAn inbox that never empties is not an inbox. It is a pile.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Never edit a file in place here. `raw/` is immutable input.\\r\\n- If something here turns out to be a duplicate of an already-archived source,\\r\\n  check by content hash (`sha256sum`), not by filename.\\r\\n- Do not delete anything from here without Joshua saying so.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b0c422","wall_time_seconds":0.4016195,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":42,"output":"kind\\tpage\\tdetail\\r\\nbroken-or-ambiguous-link\\twiki/log.md\\t../raw/README.md\\r\\nbroken-or-ambiguous-link\\twiki/log.md\\t../references/ai-workspace/expert-teaching-workflow.md\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a0fdf7","wall_time_seconds":0.3679075,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1605,"output":"# Skill parity baseline\\r\\n\\r\\nDeclared single-runtime skills. `check-skills.py` fails on **undeclared** asymmetry only:\\r\\nwhen you deliberately add a skill to one runtime and not the other, add it here in the same\\r\\ncommit. When a skill shows up as undeclared drift, either activate it for both runtimes or\\r\\ndeclare it here **with a reason**.\\r\\n\\r\\nEvery entry below carries its justification. An entry without one is not a decision, it is a\\r\\nsnapshot — which is what this file was until the audit noted below.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Audited 2026-09-12.** The Windows parity pass repaired the cross-platform validator,\\r\\ncatalogued `kling-cli` for both runtimes, and ported 25 previously undeclared Claude-only\\r\\nskills to Codex: `academy-guide`, `algorithmic-art`, `brand-guidelines`, `canvas-design`,\\r\\n`claude-api`, `claude-handoff`, `code-review`, `discernment-nudge`, `doc-coauthoring`,\\r\\n`docx`, `implement-spec`, `internal-comms`, `loop-me`, `mcp-builder`,\\r\\n`migrate-to-shoehorn`, `pdf`, `pptx`, `retro`, `session-recap`,\\r\\n`setup-ts-deep-modules`, `slack-gif-creator`, `theme-factory`,\\r\\n`web-artifacts-builder`, `wizard`, and `xlsx`. Current catalog counts:\\r\\n**claude=211 codex=188**.\\r\\n\\r\\nNote that DB parity is not activation. The DB is the complete catalog; Codex\'s activated set\\r\\nin `.agents/skills/` is a deliberately small ops core. Porting a skill here does not activate\\r\\nit, and is not meant to.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Claude only\\r\\n\\r\\n### Vendor / plugin snapshots (24) — not authored here\\r\\n\\r\\n`a11y-debugging`, `agent-development`, `build-mcp-app`, `build-mcp-server`, `build-mcpb`,\\r\\n`chrome-devtools`, `claude-automation-recommender`, `claude-md-improver`,\\r\\n`command-development`, `debug-optimize-lcp`, `discord-access`, `discord-configure`,\\r\\n`example-command`, `example-skill`, `hook-development`, `imessage-access`,\\r\\n`imessage-configure`, `math-olympiad`, `mcp-integration`, `playground`, `plugin-settings`,\\r\\n`plugin-structure`, `skill-development`, `troubleshooting`\\r\\n\\r\\nThese live under `skills/claude/_snapshots/` and ship with Claude plugins. They document\\r\\nClaude\'s own extension surfaces (hooks, plugins, MCP authoring) or vendor integrations. A\\r\\nCodex copy would describe machinery Codex does not have. Correct as Claude-only, permanently.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Claudex package: mirrored runtime directions since 2026-09-30\\r\\n\\r\\nJoshua authorized universal activation and an inverse Codex version of every skill in chaseai-yt/claudex-loop. All six package workflows are available in both catalogs and project/global activation roots. Codex uses claude-review, claude-build, grill-me-claude, and grill-with-docs-claude; Claude retains codex-review, codex-build, grill-me-codex, and grill-with-docs-codex. These deliberate name differences are paired equivalents, not missing skills. Their SKILL.md entry points deliberately differ by runtime; shared upstream supporting files remain complete.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Skill | Claude host | Codex host |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| claudex-loop | Claude plans, Codex reviews; host builds by default | Codex plans, Claude reviews; host builds by default |\\r\\n| claudex-route | Default cross-provider handoff to Codex | Default cross-provider handoff to Claude |\\r\\n| codex-review | Codex reviews, Claude revises | Claude reviews, Codex revises |\\r\\n| codex-build | Codex builds, Claude inspects | Claude builds, Codex inspects |\\r\\n| grill-me-codex | Claude interviews, Codex reviews | Codex interviews, Claude reviews |\\r\\n| grill-with-docs-codex | Same direction with context, glossary, ADRs | Inverse direction with context, glossary, ADRs |\\r\\n\\r\\nCodex entry-point names identify Claude as the delegate. The updater maps upstream names to these installed names and retires the old Codex-side copies into inactive backups. Explicit user provider/model choices still take precedence in the general loop and route. The additional grill-by-claude shortcut is intended for a Codex host.\\r\\n\\r\\nsystem/scripts/sync-claudex-skills.py updates four upstream support trees and three local entry points through the existing daily Codex automation. It computes separate runtime hashes, inserts runtime directions, and overlays the Codex review/build entry points from system/skill-overrides/claudex/codex. Local aliases remain under system/skill-overrides/claudex. Whole-tree compare-and-swap preserves local edits. Previous copies live outside activation roots under ~/.agents/claudex-backups. No source links or junctions join the vault to the clone.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Blocked by a missing Codex dependency (1)\\r\\n\\r\\n- `youtube-full` — requires a YouTube MCP server. Codex\'s `~/.codex/config.toml` declares\\r\\n  only `node_repl`, `computer-use`, `tavily`, and `notebooklm-mcp`, so the skill cannot run\\r\\n  there. **Revisit if Codex gains a YouTube MCP** — this is a capability gap, not a design\\r\\n  decision.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Claude-specific tooling (1)\\r\\n\\r\\n- `bootcamp-builder` — built around AskUserQuestion gating and subagent fan-out. A Codex copy\\r\\n  would need a rewrite, not a port.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Other ledger workflows held to one runtime (4)\\r\\n\\r\\n- `resume` — restores session state by reading the wiki ledgers (Codex uses `session-handoff`,\\r\\n  which is chat-only and writes nothing)\\r\\n- `bootcamp-parity-audit` — writes findings into the wiki\\r\\n- `morning-brief` — appends to `wiki/send-ledger.md`; also bound to a Claude-specific runner\\r\\n  (the launchd job shells out to `claude -p`)\\r\\n- `send-booking-link` — appends to `wiki/send-ledger.md` and `students/<name>/open-loops.md`\\r\\n\\r\\nThe LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows are shared by Codex and Claude as of 2026-09-30. Other ledger workflows listed here retain their declared single-runtime scope. Never have separate runtimes editing the same page concurrently.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Honest caveat:** `send-booking-link` and `morning-brief` are otherwise runtime-neutral ops\\r\\nskills. They are held back by the ledger-collision rule, not by anything they cannot do. Lowest-confidence entry in this file.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex only\\r\\n\\r\\n### Vendor / plugin skills (13)\\r\\n\\r\\n`higgsfield-generate`, `higgsfield-marketplace-cards`, `higgsfield-product-photoshoot`,\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c201d1","wall_time_seconds":0.4629129,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":28,"output":"[\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    \\"Name\\": \\"failed\\",\\r\\n    \\"Count\\": 99\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    \\"Name\\": \\"started\\",\\r\\n    \\"Count\\": 101\\r\\n  }\\r\\n]\\r\\n"}'}]

The existing one-vault architecture is usable. More tools, folders or a new graph would not fix these demonstrated problems.\n+\n+This is a partial infrastructure and routing audit using the reviewed Karpathy method, not a full semantic verification or a scored full-vault audit. Five wiki pages were inspected: Karpathy hot, rules, master index, root hot and tasks. Other evidence includes both root manuals, context, schema, connection registry, student/corpus indexes, AI workspace hub, ingest/handoff packages, parity, scheduler state, sync receipts/history, the previous audit and repair receipt. No private exports or secret values were read. No external writes, installations, repairs, deletions or scheduled jobs were triggered.\n+\n+Three confirmed defects are newly detected here: cloud combined-run failures, handoff package drift with a broken memory route, and conflicting active Karpathy compilation status. Two material verification gaps concern phone sync and current curriculum coverage. These are not an exhaustive count of every possible vault fault.\n+\n+Timestamps in metadata cover report preparation; inspection began before the first explicit clock read. Operational evidence below uses its recorded timestamps. Concurrent sync remains active; a later run can supersede these observations.\n+\n+## AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md findings\n+\n+[AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md) and [CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md) were both checked. Shared routing, permissions, authority and copy activation guidance agree in the inspected content. Runtime introductions intentionally differ. The manuals correctly route identity, teaching knowledge, student records and live systems. No operating manuals were changed.\n+\n+| File and section | Finding and practical effect | Recommended action |\n+|---|---|---|\n+| Both root manuals, Skills and Dual-runtime | Correctly require active/catalog consistency; actual handoff copies diverge (F26), a downstream package defect | Merge useful enhancements into canonical skill and correct the native memory route, then activate matching copy |\n+| Root routes to [AI workspace hub](../references/ai-workspace/index.md:19) | Active hub says full compilation remains incomplete, contradicting current Karpathy hot/native acceptance (F27) | Date or supersede the obsolete statement and link current coverage authority |\n+| Root routes to [connections](../connections.md) | Phone sync has a dated disconnection observation, not proof of present state (F22) | Recheck running Obsidian and physical phone separately |\n+\n+Scoped conventions sampled: students/README.md, LLM Wiki schema and relevant active skill instructions. Other nested manuals were not exhaustively compared.\n+\n+## Routing and migration compatibility\n+\n+| Item | Claude evidence | Codex evidence | Result |\n+|---|---|---|---|\n+| Root operating guidance | CLAUDE checked | AGENTS checked | Shared rules agree; introduction adaptation intentional |\n+| Ingest | Claude execution not retested | Project active package/schema route resolves | Static route usable; invocation not exercised today |\n+| Handoff | Broader copies not rechecked | Active/catalog diff and validator failure | F26 confirmed drift; native memory target absent |\n+| Skills overall | Catalog validator inspects both runtimes | check-skills.py exit 1, one mismatch | Inventory validation failed for handoff; not whole-library execution proof |\n+| Student records | Shared physical records | Checker passes | 23 folders, 119 local Markdown exports, 47 packages resolve |\n+| Wiki links | Shared wiki | Structural lint | 3,891 pages, two historical log links; not semantic truth proof |\n+\n+Sampled migration needs verification, not a claim that every workflow is portable. Runtime discovery/duplicate-name precedence and clean-session execution of business workflows were not rechecked. Three active Codex packages were sampled (ingest, handoff, teaching method); only handoff was independently tree-compared. Both root manuals were read. Metadata inventories are not execution evidence.\n+\n+## Routing probes\n+\n+| Question selected | Declared route | Result and limit |\n+|---|---|---|\n+| Who is Joshua and what matters? | Root to context/about-me, work, priorities | Direct. Purpose and AI workspace priority clear; July personal slate explicitly unreconfirmed |\n+| What is current work and authority? | Root to wiki/tasks and live-system authority rules | Direct to ledger. Historical app backlog is not independently revalidated current completion |\n+| What is latest Karpathy deliverable? | Root hot to native acceptance/current Karpathy hot | Direct to current lesson/hook evidence; active AI hub contradicts status (F27) |\n+| How is a source adopted? | Root schema to project ingest | Direct hash-preserving archive/compile workflow; no ingestion executed merely for audit |\n+| Where are original session records? | Root students/README and checker | Direct to local exports/packages; all tested routers resolve. Live Google source reread not performed |\n+\n+## What works\n+\n+1. Student record routing passes actual checks, preserving physical Markdown sources and local packages.\n+2. The wiki's structural lint has only two historical-log missing targets across 3,891 pages. This does not certify every statement, source hash or useful conceptual connection.\n+3. Source ownership and live-system boundaries are clear; ingest separates capture, preservation, compilation and authorized implementation.\n+\n+## Verified operational reliability\n+\n+No new numeric score is issued. This run lacks the full cross-runtime discovery, three workflow output/boundary-case checks, five probe freshness escalations and domain-specific live access evidence required to award all 20 rubric criteria. C1-C5, N1-N5, P1-P5 and D1-D5 remain unscored/null. Prior 58/100 is point-in-time historical evidence, not today's grade. Scores are not directly comparable.\n+\n+## Findings and practical impact\n+\n+### F25: Combined cloud sync is repeatedly failing (confirmed, high)\n+\n+[Runner status](../setup/github-sync/state/runner-status.json) records a failed run completed 2026-10-09T11:20:17Z, GitHub exit 1 and Drive RuntimeError. [Rolling history](../setup/github-sync/state/runner-history.json) contains 99 failed and 101 started events with no successful combined outcome in this bounded 200-event window. [GitHub status](../setup/github-sync/state/status.json) identifies antigravity-sdk-python blocked by Unapproved remote. AI OS itself is marked published, so this is not proof that all GitHub publication stopped.\n+\n+[Drive receipt](../setup/github-sync/state/drive-status.json) for AI OS remains at 2026-10-09T06:07:08Z (02:07 Toronto), commit da45eca88212294f48f3e36d4d6ad9d0dba7694c. Later failed runs do not prove current local files reached Drive. run_sync.py:61 preserves only the exception class, not a useful root-cause diagnosis. The disallowed third-party repository and the Drive exception are separate observed failures; their causal relationship is not established.\n+\n+Fix: inspect the redacted Drive error reason; explicitly exclude the unrelated tooling repository rather than approve its foreign remote; restore per-checkout publication with useful failure reporting. Complete when two distinct due runs succeed, expected current AI OS files match both destinations, and an error report identifies a recoverable cause. Do not bypass scanner/privacy checks or overwrite sync branches.\n+\n+### F26: Handoff activation diverges and points at absent memory (confirmed, medium)\n+\n+`python system/scripts/check-skills.py` exits 1 for [.agents/skills/session-handoff](../.agents/skills/session-handoff/SKILL.md) versus [catalog](../skills/codex/session-handoff/SKILL.md). Actual diff changes Claude references to Codex, producing \"Codex or Codex\" and a native memory path under ~/.Codex/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory. Test-Path for that exact memory location is false. The alternate plans directory exists, so directory absence is not claimed for plans.\n+\n+The active copy also has valuable verification timestamps and unresolved-question obligations missing from the catalog. Blindly copying the catalog would lose them. Merge those additions, correct runtime/native memory resources against current configuration, and copy the approved canonical package. Complete when validator passes and handoff retrieves intended existing state without hidden chat assumptions.\n+\n+### F27: Active AI hub contradicts current compilation status (confirmed, medium)\n+\n+[AI workspace hub](../references/ai-workspace/index.md:19) says \"Full-corpus compilation remains incomplete.\" Current [Karpathy hot](../wiki/karpathy/hot.md) and [root hot](../wiki/hot.md) state compiled sources and verified native runtime. Historical root hot sections are correctly labeled Previous, so preserving history is not itself a defect. The active hub statement lacks such qualification and can cause unnecessary rebuilding or falsely report missing setup.\n+\n+Fix: distinguish collection completeness from compilation of saved material, link the current compilation audit, and date the earlier incomplete stage. Complete when each active entry point reports the same bounded coverage and runtime state.\n+\n+### F22: Phone/remote sync remains unverified (existing, high)\n+\n+[Plan](../research/audit-repairs-2026-10-08/obsidian-sync-plan.md) observed disconnection on 2026-10-08. Parent's current read-only CLI attempt cannot find running Obsidian. Therefore current connection and phone content are unverified, not newly confirmed disconnected. GitHub/Drive backups do not substitute for Obsidian Sync. Verify exact absolute vault path, remote preservation and phone test note before connecting or deleting any vault.\n+\n+### F28: Important curriculum homes remain thin (coverage gap, medium)\n+\n+[Corpus index](../corpus/index.md) explicitly describes unrecovered topics. Metadata count today: Agree Disagree, Bootcamp, Match Family, Must Be False, Paradox, Quantifiers and Weaken have zero Markdown pages in their curriculum homes; Reading Comprehension has one. Other lecture/model libraries may contain overlapping knowledge; zero in these homes does not mean all knowledge is absent. Build a topic-to-actual-source map before importing missing source material or creating duplicate pages. Complete when needed teaching tasks resolve to an approved existing method or a clearly sourced newly compiled one.\n+\n+Optional improvement: [inbox README](../raw/inbox/README.md) says both retained processed originals are valid and \"An inbox that never empties ... is a pile.\" The operational hash contract is correct; a visible processed/unprocessed view would reduce ambiguity. This is not evidence that every listed clip is unprocessed. No automatic deletion is recommended.\n+\n+## Progress since the previous audit\n+\n+Baseline [2026-10-08 audit](audit-2026-10-08-052114-f4a92b.md), subsequent [repair receipt](../research/audit-repairs-2026-10-08/outcome.md). Findings below retain identity. Repairs reported in that receipt are documented, not silently recertified here. The current rolling sync failure is new evidence under F25 and advances the existing F23 verification concern; it is not proof of local source loss.\n+\n+## Finding ledger\n+\n+| ID | Target | Prior/repair state | Current state | Completion check |\n+|---|---|---|---|---|\n+| F01 | Original validator defects | Resolved | Not rechecked original cases;
